Walgreens Help Guide

Walgreens’s own help documentation, gathered into one screen-reader-friendly document

2026-08-03

Walgreens Help Guide

This guide gathers Walgreens’s own help as it stood on 3 August 2026. The text is Walgreens’s; nothing has been summarised or rewritten.

How this is arranged, and why it differs from the website. Walgreens does not publish one page per question. It publishes about thirty long pages, each holding a topic, a handful of sections, and a list of questions you have to unfold one at a time to read. Every one of those questions is an article here, already open, filed under the page and section Walgreens itself put it in.

On medicines. A good deal of this concerns the pharmacy, and all of it is about the service: registering, transferring a prescription, insurance and payments, auto refills, delivery, and what the text alerts mean. None of it is advice about a medicine. For anything concerning a drug itself – what it does, whether to take it, what it interacts with – ask a pharmacist. Walgreens pharmacists answer the phone for exactly that.

The surfaces it covers. The website, the Walgreens app on iPhone, iPad and Android, and Walgreens Virtual Healthcare. There is no Walgreens application for Windows: on a Windows machine the website in a browser is the product, and the website help is what applies. There is no Alexa skill documented here.

A caution about the legal pages. Walgreens’s terms of use, its privacy notices and its coupon policy are in its help centre and are kept here, but they carry no questions and are not instructions. They sit together under Policies and legal notices so they cannot be mistaken for how-to material.

Short answers are short on purpose. Many of these questions are answered in a sentence, and a few in a word. That is Walgreens’s own writing, left as it stands.

This document holds 89 categories, 621 articles and about 66,341 words.

Account: Family Prescriptions

Can a child remove access to his or her account?

Only if the child has their own Walgreens.com account with username and password. Children whose account was created through the Family Prescriptions Add a child process will not have a username and password, which are required to sign in. If the child is between ages 13 and 17 and already had an account before the Family Prescriptions account was created, he or she can sign in and remove access. Follow How do I control who manages my account? for instructions on editing account access.

Can I manage my family’s contact lens prescriptions with Family Prescriptions?

No. Family Prescriptions cannot manage contact lens prescriptions. Those customers looking to purchase contact lenses will need to sign in to their own accounts.

Can my child order prescriptions?

If a child has a Walgreens.com account with a username and password, he or she can utilize all pharmacy features on the site. Children whose account was created through the Family Prescriptions Add a child process will not have a username and password; these accounts require all online pharmacy actions to be performed by the Family Prescriptions account administrator.

How do I accept an invitation to join a Family Prescriptions account?

If someone has invited you to join a Family Prescriptions account, you will have received an email or text message as well as an email in your messages on your Account Home page. To accept the invitation:

  1. If you have an existing account click on the link in the alert or email to go to Walgreens.com. If you are not already signed in, you will be prompted to do so.
  2. Click on the link in the email or text message to view the invitation. Click “Accept invite; grant permission”.
  3. You will see a confirmation banner showing you successfully provided access to your health and prescription information to the person who sent you the invitation.

How do I add a child to my account?

To add a child to your account:

  1. Sign into your account and go to your Account Home page.
  2. Under the Profile tab, click on Family Prescriptions.
  3. Click the blue Add a child button under Who Can You Add? in the middle of the page.
  4. Fill in your child’s first and last name, date of birth, and prescription number (not contact lens prescriptions). This information must match the information on the child’s Pharmacy records.
  5. Click Continue to go to the Review and Submit page.
  6. Click Confirm if the information displayed on the page is correct, or click Edit information to make changes.

To verify that a child was added, click the Menu tab, select Settings, then select Family Prescriptions from the drop down menu. You will see your information as well as the information of the other accounts you manage. Next to Account Status you should see Full Access.

NOTE: If you are experiencing difficulty adding your child, please contact your local Walgreens pharmacy to verify the information on your child’s account. Any child (under 18 years of age) who does not already have a separate account must have a prescription filled by Walgreens in the last 18 months to be added to a Family Prescriptions account.

How do I add a pet to my account?

To add a pet to your Family Prescriptions account:

  1. Sign into your account and go to your Account Home page.
  2. Under the Profile tab click onFamily Prescriptions.
  3. Click the blue Add a pet button under Who Can You Add? in the middle of the page.
  4. Fill in your pet’s first and last name and prescription number. This information must match the information from your pet’s Pharmacy records.
  5. Click Continue to go to the Review and Submit page.
  6. Click Confirm if the information displayed on the page is correct, or click Edit information to make changes.

How do I add an adult to my account?

To add an adult to your account:

  1. Sign in to your account and click the Pharmacy & Health tab to go to your Pharmacy Home page.
  2. Under the My Pharmacy Account section on the right side of the page, click the Family Prescriptions link.
  3. Click the blue Add an adult button under Who Can You Add? in the middle of the page.
  4. Fill in the adult’s first name, last name, date of birth,and email address.
  5. Type an optional personal message in the Additional Message text field; this is a private message that will be sent to the adult you are adding to your account.
  6. Click Continue to go to the Review and Submit page.
  7. Click Confirm if the information displayed on the page is correct, or click Edit information to make changes.

An email message will be sent to invite this person to join your Family Prescriptions account on Walgreens.com. (Follow “How do I accept an invitation to join a Family Prescriptions account?” for information on how an adult accepts a Family Prescriptions account invitation.) The invitation will appear as pending until it is accepted. You will receive an email and an alert on your Account Home page once the invited adult has completed the Authorization for Release of Information to Personal Representative form and accepted your invitation.

To verify that an adult was added, click on Your Account to go to your Account Home page. Hover over the Pharmacy & Health tab and select Family Prescriptions from the drop down menu. You will see your information as well as the information of other accounts you manage. Next to Account Status, Invite Pending means the invitation was successfully sent. Full Access means the invitation has been sent and approved. If you experience difficulties, please call Customer Service toll-free, at 1-877-250-5823.

How do I control who manages my account?

To manage who has access to your account:

  1. Click on Prescriptions then click Settings and Family Prescriptions. Click Get started next to manage account access and permissions for Family Prescriptions
  2. You will see the full name of anyone who manages your Walgreens account.
  3. To turn off access to your account, select the checkbox next to the administrator’s name and click the blue Turn Off Access button.
  4. In the confirmation request popup, click Yes to remove access or No to make no changes.

How do I know how I should set up my Family Prescriptions account?

The below chart gives some helpful guidelines as to the requirements for managing your family’s prescription accounts.

Family Member Is a current Walgreens.com account required? Will an invitation be sent to the customer? Is a Walgreens Prescription Required?
18+ years old No Yes No
13-17 years old No Yes, if they have their own account Yes
12 years old and under No No Yes
Pet No No Yes

How do I remove a family member from my account?

To remove a family member from your account:

Follow the link Your Account tab to go to your Account Home page.

Locate the name of the family member whose account you no longer wish to manage and find the Account Status field. Click Remove. A confirmation box will appear.

Click Remove Family Member to confirm, or Cancel to go back to your Family Prescriptions page.

I already have an account. How do I create a Family Prescriptions account?

Sign in to your account and click the Pharmacy & Health tab to go to your Pharmacy Home page. Under the My Pharmacy Account section on the right side of the page, click the Family prescriptions link. In the middle of the page, you will see three buttons:Add an adult, Add a child, and Add a pet. Select the appropriate option and you will be taken to the corresponding page with simple instructions on how to add an account.

I am adding my child or pet and have received an invalid Prescription Number error message, but I know I am entering it correctly. What can I do?

All Walgreens prescription numbers are seven digits then a hyphen then 5 more digits. Occasionally the leading zero in the prescription number will be left off the prescription label. If your prescription is missing a digit on either side of the hyphen, try adding a zero at the beginning of the number.

I received a notification that my Family Prescriptions access is about to expire or has expired. How do I renew access?

To renew access:

-Click on the link in the email or text message to view the invitation. Click “Accept invite; grant permission”..

- You will see a confirmation banner showing you successfully provided access to your health and prescription information to the person who sent the invitation.

I received an invitation to be added to a Family Prescriptions account, but the authorization screen is blank. What can I do?

If an error occurs and you cannot accept an invitation, double check with the account administrator (who sent you the invitation) that the information he or she entered is exactly the same as on your Walgreens.com profile.

Make sure there is not another Walgreens.com account already signed in on your computer. If another user is signed in, your unique invitation link will not work. Visit any page of Walgreens.com and click Sign Out in the upper right corner, then sign in to your account.

My child is about to turn 18. What does that mean for prescription management in my Family Prescriptions account?

If your child has a Walgreens.com account with username and password, access to their account will be removed from the Family Prescriptions account administrator. For those whose account was created as a child account through the Family Prescriptions process, when the child turns 18, he or she will be removed from the family account. The Family Prescriptions account administrator will receive notice that the child has been removed. If your child wants to continue to have his or her account managed by the account administrator, the family administrator will need to re-invite the member.

What does “Invite Pending” mean?

When an adult family member or a child with a walgreens.com account is invited to join a Family Prescriptions account, he or she will receive an email or text message invitation to join that account. The invitation will be pending until it is accepted. To accept the invitation, the adult will need to click “Accept invite; grant permission” to allow pharmacy account management. The administrator will also receive notification that the invitation has been accepted. Adults will always have full control over their accounts and can opt out at any time.

What if an administrator loses access to an account?

Access to accounts can be lost if the family member denied access to their account or if the family member needs to be re-authenticated. Thirty days before the Authorization for Release of Information to Personal Representative is to expire, reminder emails are sent to the corresponding family member. It is also possible that a family member denied access to an administrator. If the administrator wants to continue to manage the account, the administrator needs to add the adult to the account again, which will send the adult a new invitation. The invitation will be pending until it is accepted. By accepting the invitation, the member agrees to give the sender permission to manage their pharmacy account.

What if two family members share an email address?

Walgreens.com accounts use your email address as the username; the same email address cannot be used for multiple Walgreens.com accounts.

You can use your shared email address as the main administrator for your Family Prescriptions account. Just follow these steps:

  1. One adult must create a separate Walgreens.com account with a different email address. If the adult does not have his or her own email address, we suggest signing up for a free account through Yahoo! Mail,Gmail, Hotmail, or check with your internet service provider.
  2. From the Walgreens.com account with the email address you share, send an invitation to the other adult. (For instructions on adding and adult to your account, follow How do I add an adult to my account?) This will make the account with your shared email address the Family Prescriptions administrator.
  3. The invited adult must sign in to the newly created email address to confirm the invitation.

Once the accounts are linked, the account administrator (your shared email address) will receive all transactional emails for each member of a Family Prescriptions account. This way, you can still utilize your shared email address to manage your prescriptions.

What is Family Prescriptions?

Family Prescriptions is a pharmacy account management tool where the family administrator can perform basic prescription services including viewing, printing, ordering prescriptions, and basic account management for minors and other adults (based on permission). Information on your Family Prescriptions account is located under the My Pharmacy Account section on your Pharmacy Home page. To add an adult, administrators must invite the adult through the Family Prescriptions Add an adult link. Children and pets that have had prescriptions filled at Walgreens in the last 18 months can be added with basic information and a prescription number through the Add a child or Add a pet links. Please note: all adults must have a Walgreens.com account. Invited adult members without an account can create one through a link in the invitation email.

What’s required to add a child or pet to my Family Prescriptions account?

If the child or pet does not already have a Walgreens.com account, you can add them simply with Name, Date of Birth and Walgreens prescription number from the last 18 months. Please note, the information you enter must match what is on the prescription label. If you notice any discrepancies with the information on the label that must be corrected, please contact your local Walgreens store.

What’s required to add an adult to my Family Prescriptions account?

If the invited member has a Walgreens.com account you will be required to enter the name, date of birth, and phone number exactly as it appears on the member’s account. Or an active prescription number from the past 18 months can be entered.

Account: General Troubleshooting Information

How can clearing cookies assist with technical troubleshooting?

Clearing cookies basically gives you the ability to restart your browser with all fresh information so that it is not using old information to control your online session.

How can I clear the cookies or cache on my computer?

To ensure you have the most current instructions on how to clear the cookies or cache on your internet browser, please use the links below to access the support section of the browser that you are using. Please note that once you access the links below, you will need to search the terms “clear cookie” or “clear cache” to find information related to that topic. Generally, the search box is located at the top right of the page.

  • Apple, Safari:https://www.apple.com/support/safari
  • Google, Chrome:https://www.google.com/support/chrome
  • Microsoft, Edge:https://support.mozilla.com

How do cookies affect my session on walgreens.com?

They permit you to take full advantage of walgreens.com features and they record your activity on the site in order to provide better service when you return to our site.

How does cache affect my session on walgreens.com?

By using cache to open the site, you are not getting the most current version of the site. Normally, this would be fine, but we frequently do updates to our site. So, if updates were made to the site, you will not see the changes because you don’t have the most current version of the page. You will not be able to see the changes without clearing the cache on your computer.

If I clear my cookies, do I have to erase all of my saved passwords for other sites I visit frequently?

No, you do not. You can customize which cookies you are going to remove before you complete the process.

Is it a requirement to clear my cookies or cache?

No, this is your choice. This is merely a suggestion by Walgreens to assist customers with technical issues on the site.

What are cookies?

Cookies are small files that are stored on your computer that remember information about your preferences; such as passwords and other user specific information. They allow you to move within our website without having to log in at every page you visit on the site.

What is cache?

It is a means of helping to improve how fast data is opened while browsing the internet. In most cases, each time a web page is opened, the content is sent to your temporary internet files. The next time you visit a page that you have previously opened, it will open from the cache rather than downloading the content on the page again.

What should I do if clearing cookies or cache has not resolved the technical issue?

Please contact our customer service department for additional assistance by calling 877-250-5823.

Account: Registration

Are there requirements for my Walgreens.com password?

Yes, when you create your account or reset your password it must meet the following requirements:

  • - Minimum 8 characters; at least 1 letter and 1 number; case-sensitive.
  • - Must contain alphabetic and numeric characters. This requirement is waived if password is 16 or more characters.
  • - Must be different than your username, first name, last name, or email address.
  • - Cannot contain patterns or common keyboard combinations like abcd1234, qwertyuiop1, asdf1234, etc.
  • - Cannot contain a commonly used password like password, letmein, etc. Cannot be the same as any previous password.

Can I create an account for my entire family?

Yes. A Family Prescriptions account features an administrator who manages the accounts of linked family members or others under your care. With Family Prescriptions, you can order new prescriptions, refill existing prescriptions or transfer prescriptions. For information on creating and managing a Family Prescriptions account, follow Help: Family Prescriptions.

Can I register for Walgreens.com at my Walgreens store?

Yes, your Walgreens store can start an account for you. Just provide your email address at the pharmacy and you will receive an email with a registration link. Select the email message to get started. To be a fully registered pharmacy customer, you will be prompted to follow the security steps for verification, providing an extra level of security on your pharmacy account.

Can others view my personal information?

All your information, including prescription, health and credit card transactions, will be securely stored and can only be accessed by you and authorized Walgreens personnel.

Can you give me an example of a typical password?

The most secure passwords consist of letters, numbers and symbols such as: !, #, $, %, etc. For example, SaveBig$10!

Do I have to be a registered customer to shop Walgreens.com?

No, you don’t have to be registered. You can skip registration and proceed directly to checkout, but you will not be able to track or save your order. To purchase items without registering, simply select “Check out as a guest”.“

How do I pick a password?

Passwords must be at least eight characters long (cAsE sEnSiTiVe) and must include one number. Passwords may also include letters, numbers and other symbols such as !, #, $, %, etc. The most secure passwords combine all three different character types.

How do I register?

Follow the Create Your Account link. Fill in your first and last name, your email address that you will use to sign in to Walgreens.com, and a password of your choosing. Click the blue Continue button to submit your information.

Why do I need to provide personal information in order to register as a pharmacy customer on Walgreens.com?

Your personal info is used to correctly link your Walgreens.com account and your pharmacy profile and protect your phi, etc. Once saved, this information will be secure, accessed only by you and authorized Walgreens personnel.

Why do you ask for my phone number?

Your phone number helps us further verify your account.

Why do you need my date of birth and gender?

Your date of birth and gender is required to verify your account.

Why should I register?

Registering on our site gives you access to a wide variety of Walgreens services that will simplify your shopping experience and make it more enjoyable. You can check your order history, take advantage of photo services and, of course, manage your prescriptions.

Account: Sign In

How do I unlock my account?

Once your account is locked, you have three options to gain access again:

  1. If you think you have remembered your password you can wait 15 minutes until it unlocks and try again.
  2. You can request a link to reset your password.
  3. You can call customer care at 877-250-5823.

What does the “Update Username” popup mean?

If, when you signed in to your Walgreens.com account, you received a message that says “Update Username” it is because you are using a username that is not a unique email address. Walgreens.com is transitioning to identifying accounts by a unique email address. The “Update Username” box is a quick and easy way to confirm your email address as your username.

Select the button next to Use Your Email Address (recommended) to confirm your username as the email address that is currently associated with your account. Click Save Settings to confirm. This is the recommended setting for all users.

Select the button next to Use Different Email Address and type an updated email address into the text box to change your username to a different email address. Click Save Settings to confirm. This will also change the default email address for any email subscriptions and communications you receive from Walgreens.

Select the button next to Keep Current Username to continue to use your current username (not your email address) to sign in. Click Save Settings to confirm, or Cancel to continue without making changes.

If this does not resolve your issue, please contact Customer Service toll-free at 1-877-250-5823.

What happens if I try the wrong password too many times?

If you enter an incorrect password more than six (6) times your account will be locked for 15 minutes as a security precaution. Once your account is locked, you will have the option to reset your password by either requesting a link to reset your password or calling Customer Care at 877-250-5823.

What if I forget my password, username or email address?

If you have forgotten your password:

- Click Forgot your password? under the Sign In box. You will be taken to the Reset Password page. Reset Password page.

- Enter your username and click Continue to reset your password.

- You will have the option to have a verification code emailed or texted to you.

- Check your email/text. You will receive a verification code. Enter the code and click Continue.

- Or you can click the link in the email or text to create a new password. This will bring you to the Reset Password page.

- Create a new password and type it into the boxes.

- Click Confirm to set your new password.

You can now sign in to Walgreens.com with your new password.

If you have forgotten your username or email address:

- If you registered for a Walgreens.com account on July 15, 2010 or after, your email address is your username.

- If you were a Walgreens customer prior to July 15, 2010:

  • - On the Sign In or Register page, click Forgot your username? under the Username box. You will be taken to the Retrieve Username page.
  • - Enter your email address in the text box and click Submit. An email with your username will be sent to you with the subject “Retrieve Username.”

-NOTE: If you do not receive the Retrieve Username email, please check your email’s Spam or Junk folder.

- If you have forgotten the email address associated with your account, please contact Customer Service toll-free at 1-877-250-5823.

When I enter my username and password, I am prompted to enter them again. Why?

Our site requires that the user’s web browser accepts cookies. If your browser is set to refuse cookies, or if you have disabled them, you will be asked to sign in every time you click a link that requires registration on our site. To avoid this, you should enable cookies on your browser so that our site recognizes you throughout your visit.

About Cookies: Cookies are small files that your browser places on your computer’s hard drive. They enable our site to know whether you are a returning customer and if you found Walgreens.com via another site or advertisement. The cookies help us personalize your shopping experience and save you from re-entering information each time you visit. Cookies do not enable us to determine the identity of any visitor to our web site. We cannot identify you unless you specifically tell us who you are. Most Internet browsers permit you to erase or block cookies.

The instructions to change the cookie setting are different for every browser and browser version. Please refer to the Help menu of your browser for further instructions. If this doesn’t resolve your issue, please call Customer Service at 1-866-250-5823.

Why does Walgreens want me to use my email address to sign in?

Custom usernames are often easily forgotten and different for each website someone might use. Using an email address to sign in provides an easy-to-remember and unique identifier for website users. If you created your account before July 15, 2010 and registered using a username that is not a unique email address, you will be asked over the next few months to convert your username to an email address.

What browsers does Walgreens.com support?

Walgreens.com supports the following browsers:

  • Microsoft, Edge
  • Mozilla Firefox 99 and above
  • Google Chrome 99 and above
  • Safari 12.1 and above

If your browser and version is not listed, we recommend using a supported browser. Otherwise, pages may not display correctly and certain features may not work.

Several features of our site require installation of third-party applications, including Adobe Acrobat, Flash, and Java. These are available at no charge to you.

To install any of these applications, select the appropriate icon and follow the instructions to install them on your computer.

button next to Refill Reminders.

You will see a confirmation message that you’ve successfully updated your preferences.

How can I edit my communication preferences?

To edit your communication preferences:

  1. Follow the link Your Account to go to your Account Home page.
  2. Hover over the Personal Settings tab and select Communication Preferences from the drop down menu. You will be directed to the Manage Communication Preferences page. Here, you can edit your text message alerts, automated phone calls, email reminders, email subscriptions, and your personal reminders.
  3. Next to each section on this page, you will see either a bright blue Edit, Sign Up or Add Personal Reminder button. Click this button to edit the information in that section.
  4. Click the blue Save Changes button when you have made your changes. You will see a confirmation message that you’ve updated your communication preferences.

How can I edit my email subscriptions?

To edit email subscription options:

  1. Follow the link Your Account to go to your Account Home page.
  2. Click the Emails and Alerts tab.
  3. Scroll down to Your Email Subscriptions.
  4. To subscribe to an email, check the corresponding box. To stop receiving an email, uncheck the corresponding box.
  5. Click the blue Save Changes button when you have made your changes. You will see a confirmation message that you’ve successfully updated your email preferences.

How can I edit my text message/automated phone alerts?

To edit text message preferences:

  1. Follow the link Your Account to go to your Account Home page.
  3. Click on Emails and Alerts tab.
  4. Under the Pharmacy & Health Options click the blue Edit button next to the option you want to update.
  5. Click the blue Save Changes button when you have made your changes.
  6. You will see a confirmation message that you’ve successfully updated your preferences.
  7. To sign up for Rx alert text messages, select Yes under the question Would you like to receive text message Rx Alerts? If you would prefer not to receive text messages, select No.
  8. Click the blue Save Changes button when you have made your changes.
  9. You will see a confirmation message that you’ve successfully updated your text message preferences.

NOTE: If you have not made any changes, you will not receive a confirmation message. Click Cancel to return to the Emails and Alerts page.

  4. You will required to give consent to receive text or phone calls.
  5. Click the blue Save Changes button when you have made your changes. You will see a confirmation message that you’ve successfully updated your preferences.

  4. Select the checkbox next to each message you’d like to delete and click the bright blue Delete button. You will see a Confirm Your Request window that asks: Are you sure you want to delete the selected messages?

How do I save a store location in my preferences?

To save a store location:

  1. Follow the link Your Account to go to your Account Home page.
  2. Hover over the Personal Settings tab and click My Walgreens Store from the drop down menu. If you have previously selected a preferred store, you’ll see it here. Click the blue Change Store button to select a new store. You will be directed to the Store Locator page.
  3. My marital status has changed. How do I update my name?

What happened to the security question?

Security questions are no longer used to access your account. You can verify your identity using other options, such as receiving a one-time code by text, email, or confirming details from your prescription information (if applicable). To ensure a smooth experience, please make sure your email address and phone number on file are up to date.

Why does my pharmacy email not include details about my prescriptions?

For your security, by default, prescription refill and pharmacy transactional emails do not include prescription names, numbers, or dosage information.

Will I receive a message when my doctor or clinic denies a refill?

Yes, you will receive a message that says your prescription is not ready and that we did not receive approval from your provider.

Checkout: Checkout Process

How can I redeem credits or coupon codes?

To redeem a coupon code for a special offer or discount, it must be entered at the time of checkout. On the Checkout page, enter the coupon code in the Have a special promotion or coupon code? box at the bottom of the left navigation column of your Shopping Cart page. Make sure to check the Apply link (in bright blue) next to the box before you select the Proceed to Checkout button in bright blue, near the bottom of the right navigation column. The discounted product price will be displayed at checkout and in your Order Summary below Promotions applied to your order. From time to time, you may receive a credit to your Photo Center account. If you do, we will automatically place it in your Photo Cart for you.

How do I checkout?

Once everything is in your Shopping Cart, select from pickup in store, same day delivery or ship to home. Then click Proceed to Checkout button to begin the checkout process.

  1. Complete the Shipping information or choose the store location and click Continue to Checkout.
  2. Complete the Payment information and click Continue to Review.
  3. Review your order summary on the Review & Submit Order page. If everything is correct, click the blue Submit Order button at the bottom of the page.

Once your order goes through, you will see your Order Confirmation. You can print your receipt by clicking the blue Print Receipt button. Also, a copy of your receipt will be emailed to you.

My buy one, get one FREE products are not showing up at checkout. Why?

Most likely the reason the products are not showing up is because you only placed one applicable product in your cart. In order to get buy one, get one FREE products, you need to place two of the applicable products in your cart.

Why did I receive a credit card error message during my checkout?

The reason for the credit card error is a credit authorization failure by your financial institution. This may be due to one of the following reasons: insufficient funds, credit card expiration or the billing address you provided does not match the company (i.e. Visa or Master Card) files. Please contact your bank for further assistance.

Checkout: Tax & Tax Exempt

Do you process tax exempt orders?

We’re sorry, but we cannot process tax exempt transactions online. If you are eligible for tax exempt purchases, please visit your local Walgreens.

Please note that stores cannot offer tax exempt purchases for online orders.

Sign up for deals and offers!

Why must I pay tax on an online order?

Sales taxes will be charged at the applicable rate based on the ship-to address, except for orders shipped to Illinois. For orders shipped to Illinois, internet sales tax is charged based on the point of order acceptance, by law. The Walgreens.com point of acceptance is Mount Prospect, IL. The tax amount will appear on the order confirmation page.

This is where you can enter your personal information and upload and submit a Microsoft Word version of your resume. Each job description will display an Apply Now button that will walk you through the steps of applying for a position online.

Once you select a category, you can search by location or job category and view a list of all positions available in your selected category or location and click on any position to view a detailed job description. If you are applying for a local position you may be asked for your zip code and other information to narrow your search to your local area.

Company Info: Walgreens Company

Does Walgreens participate in any charitable efforts?

The Walgreen Company funds charities through a corporate giving program with an emphasis on existing programs for mentoring and tutoring of primary school children, single disease agencies and fundraising efforts in our stores for our three national charities: the American Cancer Society, Juvenile Diabetes Research Foundation and the American Heart Association. Limited funding for other programs goes primarily to domestic, non-hospital-based healthcare issues. To learn more about submitting a request, see our Charitable Donations page, Contribution Guidelines or call 1-847-315-4663.

How can I contact Walgreens Media Relations?

5.9 million daily. Browse Financial and Other Numbers for the latest figures.

How many employees work for Walgreen Co.?

The Walgreen Company employs over 240,000 people with diverse education, training and backgrounds whose mission is to serve our patients and customers every day. Browse Financial and Other Numbers for the latest figures.

How many prescriptions do you fill each year?

Walgreens filled 287.6 million prescriptions (including immunizations) on a 30 day adjusted basis in fiscal year 2020. Browse Financial and Other Numbers for the latest figures.

How many Walgreens stores are there?

We have thousands of stores in the contiguous 48 states, Alaska, Hawaii, the District of Colombia and Puerto Rico. As the nation’s leading pharmacy retailer, Walgreens is experiencing one of the most dramatic growth rates in the industry. For the latest figures, follow Stores. In addition to its drugstores, Walgreens also operates work site health centers, home care facilities and specialty, institutional and mail service pharmacies.

Is a corporate organization chart available?

You can view our Board of Directors and Corporate Officers here. For proprietary reasons, we do not provide detailed information on specific topics, such as our corporate strategy, internal systems and corporate structure.

Is Walgreens an eco-friendly company?

W-Greens is one of our newest business groups. Their mission is to support our environmental initiatives. Visit Social Responsibility for more details.

What are Walgreens company values?

At Walgreens, our values are based on the same principles that the company was founded on more than a century ago:

Honesty, trust, and integrity with our customers, our shareholders, suppliers,the communities we serve, and among ourselves.

Quality through consistent and reliable service, advice, and products across every touchpoint and channel.

Caring, compassionate and driven to delivering a great customer and patient experience through outstanding service and a desire for healthy outcomes.

A strong community commitment and presence built through service, expertise, and the personal engagement of every Walgreen team member.

What is the Walgreens Brand Guarantee?

Your satisfaction is 100% guaranteed on all Walgreens brand products! If you are not completely satisfied, return the unused portion and we will refund the full price.

What is the Walgreens Corporate Vision Statement?

Walgreens Vision Statement

To be “My Walgreens” for everyone in America - the first choice in health and daily living… owning the strategic territory of “well”.

Coupons: Paperless Coupons

Can I clip coupons from the weekly ad using keywords?

At this time you can only clip manufacturer coupons and coupons from the monthly savings book. The weekly ad coupons will be added at a later date. You can clip the weekly ad coupons online or on the mobile app.

Can I use a promo code and a manufacturer coupon on the same order or item?

Yes. You can use a promo code and manufacturer coupon on the same order or item.

Do I have a myWalgreens account to take advantage of Paperless Coupons?

Yes, you must have a myWalgreens account in order to use Paperless Coupons. If you do not have one, you can sign up here.

How can I tell if coupons have been added to my profile?

On the Paperless Coupons homepage, you can view a list of all the coupons that are currently clipped to your myWalgreens account by clicking on the clipped coupons button. You can also see coupons soon to expire and redeemed coupons by clicking on the corresponding buttons.

How do I clip coupons on the mobile app?

To clip coupons via the Walgreens mobile app:

  1. Select the Weekly Ad & Coupons icon from the home screen of the Walgreens app.
  2. Select Start Clipping from the Weekly Ad & Coupon page.
  3. Available coupons will display on the next screen. Click Clip under the offer to add it to your myWalgreens account. You may also click on the offer to display coupon details and then clip the offer from the next screen.

How do I find Paperless Coupon offers?

Once you are logged into your myWalgreens account, please visit Walgreens offers page at walgreens.com/coupons. Here, you will find several coupon offers for you to browse through and search. Simply click on the Clip to Card button to add a particular offer to your account. You can search by keyword in the box at the top right of the page.

You can also clip directly from the online version of the Weekly Ad. After navigating to the Weekly Ad (by clicking the link at the top of any page on Walgreens.com or the link on the left side of the Paperless Coupons page) hover over the coupon you would like to clip and click the Clip to Card button.

How do I know which coupons were clipped?

Click on the link that is provided in the text message that you receive back from Walgreens.

How do I know which products are eligible for each coupon?

Check the coupon details or click on the shop link for that coupon to see a list of eligible products.

How do I redeem my coupons in the store?

At checkout just use your phone number that is linked to your myWalgreens account and the coupons will be automatically applied.

How do I redeem my coupons on my Walgreens.com order?

If you place an order on Walgreens.com using the account that is linked to your myWalgreens account the coupons will automatically apply.

How do I remove coupons from my myWalgreens account?

Click on clipped coupons. This will give you a list of the active coupons that you have clipped. You can remove a coupon from your account by clicking the unclip button at the lower right corner of the product description. Once a coupon is unclipped, it can no longer be clipped, so unclip with caution. Coupons that have been added to your myWalgreens account will be automatically applied at the cash register and removed from the account.

Expired coupons that were clipped to your account will be removed from your account when the expiration date passes. The expiration date will be shown at the top of the product description. Coupons expire at 11:59 P.M. in the time zone of the store where they are redeemed.

How many times can I redeem a coupon?

Each coupon is allowed to be redeemed once. Once it is redeemed, the coupon will be removed from your clipped coupons list and move into the redeemed coupons section.

NOTE: Single use coupons can be redeemed only once, either digital or paper, but not both.

If I use a paperless coupon on an item that I later return, what happens to my coupon?

We will refund the amount that you paid for the item plus the value of the coupon. The coupon itself will not be returned.

Sign up for deals and offers!

Is there a limit to how many coupons I can have clipped to my account at one time?

Yes, you will see a warning message at the top of the Paperless Coupons homepage when you are approaching the clipped coupon limit.

What are Paperless Coupons?

Walgreens Paperless Coupons are electronic coupons that you can clip directly to your myWalgreens account. You can save offers directly to your myWalgreens account from your mobile phone using the Walgreens app or at walgreens.com/coupons from your computer. These digital coupons are automatically applied in-store, at checkout, when you use your myWalgreens account, enter your phone number, or have the sales associate scan your phone through the Walgreens mobile app.

Full coupon policies can be found here.

What happens to my paperless coupon if my order is canceled?

If your order is canceled for any reason, we’ll return all applied coupons back to your clipped coupons. We can’t return promo codes at this time.

What if I used a paperless coupon on an item and the item is out of stock?

If your item is out of stock and you don’t want us to substitute an item, or one is unavailable, we’ll return the coupon and it will reappear in your clipped coupons.

What is Text2Clip and how does it work?

Text2Clip allows myWalgreens members to text a keyword to 41456 to receive paperless coupons related to that keyword. The coupons will automatically be clipped to your account and redeemed at checkout.

Why am I unable to redeem my coupon at the checkout?

Please ensure you have read the offer purchase requirements carefully to ensure the brand, quantity, size, and price match with the item you are trying to purchase. If everything matches and you are still having trouble redeeming the coupon, please contact Walgreens.com Customer Service at 1-877-250-5823 with the product description and UPC product barcode number.

NOTE: National manufacturer coupons will only be available for online redemption in Puerto Rico stores.

Why did I receive a message to call customer service?

You either have multiple myWalgreens accounts that we are unable to identify or we cannot find your account. Customer Service can help to ensure you have one valid account.

Why was I suspended from clipping coupons for 12 hours?

If you text the same keyword more than 4 times your phone number will be suspended for 12 hours. Please try again after 12 hours has passed.

Will the coupons apply for my online ship to you orders?

Yes. Paperless manufacturer coupons can be used on ship to you orders.

Coupons: Printable Coupons

Can I use manufacturer’s coupons for online purchases?

Yes. Manufacturer coupons available for a product will be available to clip directly from the product page.

Can I use multiple coupon codes in one order?

It depends. If the coupon codes you’re applying are for different products (e.g. one code for 15% off fragrances and one code for $20 off contact lenses), then the shopping cart will accept multiple coupon codes. However, if the coupon codes are for the same product (e.g. one code for $20 off contacts and one code for 15% off contacts) and/or stacked on top of a site-wide promo (e.g. one code for 15% off Walgreens brand vitamins and one code for $10 off a $50 purchase), the shopping cart will not accept multiple coupon codes.

What guidelines does Walgreens have for coupon usage?

As a customer-focused retailer, Walgreens encourages the use of coupons by our customers in our retail stores, in accordance with our Coupon Policy Guidelines. These guidelines include information from Sale Items, Multiple Coupons, Buy One, Get One Free Coupons, Internet/Print at Home Coupons, Earning Register RewardsTM, and Redeeming Register RewardsTM.

Gift Card: Bulk Gift Cards

Do I need to activate the plastic Gift Cards I received from my online order?

If your order is under $1,999.99, no action is needed, and the cards will arrive active and ready to use. If your order is $2,000.00 or larger, you will need to activate the gift cards via the portal.

How can I check my gift card balance?

If your card number starts with 627857101 or 627857105 please call 1-866-922-7312. If you have a gift card that starts with any other number please go to Walgreens Retail Card or call 1-833-522-2487.

  • Click on My Orders
  • Click Filters
  • Search for your Order Number and select the radio button next to that Order in the search results
  • Click the Action dropdown
  • Select Activate
  • The plastic gift cards will be activated within 8 hours

How do I reset/change my password?

Change your password in My Profile, under My Account. If you have forgotten your password, click the “Forgot My Password”, enter your email address and an email will be sent with a link to reset your password.

How do I update my information (address, phone number, ID expiration date, etc.)

Individuals: update your information in My Profile, under My Account. As part of Walgreens ongoing efforts to prevent financial services-related fraud and money laundering, changes will not be made until the information submitted is verified.

Businesses: Please send an email to WalgreensBulkSupport@InComm.com with the requested changes on company letterhead. Once the information is verified, a specialist will make the requested changes to the account.

How long does it take to receive my order?

Once payment is received and confirmed, it can take up to 48 hours for the order to be fulfilled. You will receive a confirmation email once the order has been fulfilled. Transit times are:

  • Standard Ground: 3-5 days after fulfillment
  • Expedited 2 Day: 2 days after fulfillment

If I pay for an order with a Wire Transfer, ACH or Check and my order is cancelled, how will the funds be returned?

There are no returns at this time. Please contact 833-925-8625.

What are the benefits of a Bulk Gift Card Account?

Individuals: A Bulk Gift Card account provides the ability to purchase over $750 in gift cards within one online transaction.

Businesses: A Bulk Gift Card account provides the ability to purchase over $750 in gift cards within one transaction, the ability to pay with a WIRE transfer, ACH or Check, and the ability to add authorized users to the account with their own login credentials.

All our gift card orders are shipped via trackable service. You will find the tracking information in the Items Ordered section of the Order Details screen.

Who can apply for a Walgreens Bulk Gift Card Account?

Individuals: with a valid government-issued ID and valid Social Security number can apply for an Individual Account. You will need to complete an application with basic information about yourself (e.g. name, DOB, Social Security number, contact information, etc.), and agree to our Walgreens Gift Card Terms and Conditions and Walgreens Corporate Gift Cards Bulk Purchase Agreement.

Businesses: with a valid Taxpayer Identification Number or Employer Identification Number can apply for a Bulk Gift Card Account. You will need to complete an application with basic information about your business (e.g. business license number, tax ID number, contact information, etc.) and agree to our Walgreens Gift Card Terms and Conditions, our Walgreens Corporate Gift Cards Bulk Purchase Agreement, as well as acknowledge you’ve read and understood our Fraud brochure (Partner Fraud Policy).

Who do I contact with additional gift card questions?

The Bulk Gift Card Team is available Monday – Friday from 9:00 AM – 5:00 PM CST

  • By phone at 833-925-8625
  • By email at WalgreensBulkSupport@incomm.com
  • Closed major holidays

Sign up for deals and offers!

Why does the account require a vetting process?

The U.S. Bank Secrecy Act, designed to prevent the funding of terrorism and money laundering activities, requires Incomm, a Walgreens service provider, to verify the identities of customers purchasing large amounts of prepaid access products (i.e., gift cards). Your information will be vetted by a specialist to determine eligibility.

Why is signature required on my FedEx Shipment?

As an extra layer of security for our customers, we are requiring signature delivery for all orders over $1,000.

Will my credit be impacted by the Bulk Application Process?

No, we do not inquire data from nor report data to credit bureaus.

Yes, Walgreens Gift Cards can be used for retail prescription purchases.

Can Walgreens Gift Cards be reloaded/can funds be added?

No, Walgreens Gift Cards cannot be reloaded, nor can funds be added. A new gift card must be purchased.

Mobile and Social Media: Mobile Messaging

Can I shop Walgreens and check out with my mobile device?

Yes! You can shop, check out, and ship products to your home with all of our mobile applications as well as our mobile optimized site.

How can I order prescriptions refills from my cell phone?

To refill prescriptions from your phone:

  1. Go to m.walgreens.com on your phone’s mobile browser.
  2. Select the Pharmacy tab.
  3. Choose either the Refill Prescriptions (for delivery or pickup) or Express Refills (for store pickup only) button to view prescriptions that are available for refill.

NOTE: If you are not already logged in, you will be prompted to enter your username and password.

How can I reset my mobile password?

Follow Reset Password. This will reset the password for all your Walgreens services.

How do I sign up for mobile messaging?

New customers can sign-up for Walgreens Mobile Messaging during their registration process. Follow Text Alerts to set up this service. To sign up for Prescription Text Alerts, click the blue Edit Text Message Preferences link in the left column. To sign up for Special Offers texts, click the blue Sign Up Now link in the right column.

How do I stop Walgreens mobile messaging from coming to my cell phone?

To opt out at any time, text the word STOP to 21525.

I am having trouble receiving messages. How can I get help?

You must respond to the Rx Alerts welcome text message within 48 hours to complete the sign up process and verify that you have signed up for the program. If you have completed this step and are still unable to receive messages from a store that is equipped with messaging services, contact your local store. If related to Walgreens.com Online Retail Pharmacy, please contact Customer Service toll-free, at 1-877-250-5823. If related to Walgreens Mail Service, please contact Mail Service toll-free, at 1-800-345-1985.

I don’t have a Walgreens photo or pharmacy account. Can I create an account from my mobile device?

Absolutely. You can easily sign up for a photo and/or pharmacy account with all of our mobile apps.

What are the participating carriers for the Walgreens Mobile program?

Participating carriers include the following: Alltel, AT&T, Boost, Cellular One, Cellular South, Cincinnati Bell, Nextel, Sprint, T-Mobile, U.S. Cellular, Verizon Wireless, Virgin Mobile, Bluegrass, Centennial Wireless, ECIT, EKN, Immix, Inland Cellular, Illinois Valley Cellular, Revol and West Central Wireless.

What is Walgreens mobile messaging?

Walgreens mobile messaging is a service for registered customers to receive text alerts* delivered to a mobile device. These messages include Pharmacy Alerts, which will send you prescription refill notifications, and Special Offers, which offers exclusive deals, coupons and more.

NOTE: Pharmacy Alerts and Special Offers are separate subscriptions. You can sign up for one, both, or neither.

*Standard text message and data rates from your carrier or plan may apply. Walgreens does not charge a fee for this service.

Mobile and Social Media: Prescription Text Notifications

Can I just choose specific medications for 90 day refills?

If you want to pick specific medications for 90 day refills, please contact your local pharmacist. Otherwise, if you have received a text message with the option to refill your prescriptions for 90 days, and you respond with the RX90 keyword, those prescriptions that are due for refill will be requested for a 90 day fill.

Do I have to get 90 day refills through the mail service?

No, you don’t have to use mail service. If you choose 90 day fills, you can pick up your prescriptions at your local pharmacy.

How can I get more information about my prescription I received a text notification about?

If you have any questions about a prescription you received a text notification about, please call the number included in your text notification to contact your pharmacy where the prescription was filled.

How much will my 90 day refill prescriptions cost?

There is a possibility that paying for your prescription once every 90 days will help save on co-pays. The cost of your prescription refill depends on your insurance policy. You’ll need to contact your local pharmacy for your co-pay amount once your eligible prescriptions are refilled.

I want to sign up for prescriptions text alerts. What do I do?

To sign up for prescription text alerts:

  1. Follow the link Your Account to go to your Account Home page.
  2. Hover over the Settings tab and select Communication Preferences from the dropdown menu. You will be directed to the Manage Communication Preferences page.
  3. In the box labeled Text Message Alerts, you’ll see the question “Do you want to receive Pharmacy Prescription Ready Alerts?” Select Yes.
    NOTE: If you have not saved a cell phone number in your Personal Information, click Add a number in the bottom left corner of the Text Message Alerts box.
  4. Click Save Changes. You will see a confirmation message that you’ve successfully updated your text message preferences.
    NOTE: If you have not made any changes, the Save Changes button will not be clickable.

What are the benefits of 90 day refills?

You’ll save time by making fewer trips to the pharmacy. Plus, you’ll potentially save on co-pays depending on your insurance.

When will my 90 day refill be available?

Orders received today should be ready by noon tomorrow. You’ll receive a text message when your order is ready. We’ll contact you if there are any delays.

Which medications are eligible for 90 day refills?

Most maintenance medications are eligible - excluding controlled substance prescriptions. If you have questions about prescription eligibility, please contact your local pharmacy.

Will you contact my doctor regarding my 90 day refill request?

We may need to contact your doctor depending on the state you live in (State Regulations) and/or your insurance plan.

myWalgreens: Balance® Rewards

What happened to Balance® Rewards?

We ended the Balance Rewards program to give you something even better! myWalgreens is a one-of-a-kind personalized experience that makes saving, shopping and your well-being easier.

What happens to my Balance® Rewards points if they aren’t used by April 16, 2021?

Balance Rewards points converted to Walgreens Cash rewards expired on April 16, 2021 if the Balance Rewards member did not register for myWalgreens™ by then. If you join myWalgreens™ after April 16, 2021, rewards previously earned as a Balance Rewards member will not be reinstated. Join myWalgreens now to unlock sale prices, earn rewards and more—it’s free, fast and easy.

Why did we change from Balance® Rewards points to Walgreens Cash rewards?

We heard your feedback and simplified our rewards from Balance Rewards points to Walgreens Cash rewards, to make earning and tracking rewards easier for you. Plus, new Walgreens Cash rewards are equal to the value you earned as points—10 Balance Rewards points or 1% Walgreens Cash rewards on most purchases—so you’ll keep saving at the same great rate when you shop eligible items. $1 Walgreens Cash rewards is equivalent to 1000 Balance Rewards points, and redeeming $1 in Walgreens Cash rewards is the equivalent to saving $1 on your eligible purchase.

(1) 1000 points = $1 rewards

(2) 2000 points = $2 rewards

(3) 3000 points = $3 rewards

(4) 4000 points = $4 rewards

(5) 5000 points = $5 rewards

(6) 10,000 points = $10 rewards

(7) 20,000 points = $20 rewards

(8) 50,000 points = $50 rewards

myWalgreens: Eligibility & Joining

Can anyone sign up for myWalgreens™?

myWalgreens™ is available to U.S. residents at least 16 years old, and it’s free to join! Members between 16 and 18 years old (or between 16 and 21 years of age in Puerto Rico) must get permission from their parent or legal guardian first.

Can Walgreens reinstate my rewards if I join myWalgreens after April 16, 2021?

No, you can only keep your rewards if you join myWalgreens™ by April 16, 2021. That’s why it’s important to sign up for myWalgreens by then, so you can keep your current rewards balance and earn even more.

What do I need to redeem my Walgreens Cash rewards?

Your email or home address is required to redeem Walgreens Cash rewards. The best way to access personalized deals and additional program benefits is by adding your email address to your profile.

What happens if I was a Balance® Rewards member and join myWalgreensTM after April 16, 2021?

The Balance Rewards program has ended along with all membership benefits. If you join myWalgreens™ after April 16, 2021, rewards previously earned as a Balance Rewards member will not be reinstated. Join myWalgreens now to unlock sale prices, earn rewards and more—it’s free, fast and easy.

What if I don’t want to provide my email address?

No problem! You can still unlock sale price and earn Walgreens Cash rewards by providing your phone number, ZIP code and name. You can redeem your rewards by providing either your email or home address.

What if I was a Balance® Rewards member and didn’t sign up for myWalgreens™ by April 16, 2021?

You can still join myWalgreens to regain access to storewide savings, only-for-you deals and product recommendations. Join free now, it’s fast and easy.

What is required to become a myWalgreens™ member?

Your first name, last name, ZIP code, and phone number are needed to join myWalgreens™. Providing this basic information allows you to unlock sale prices in store and start earning Walgreens Cash rewards that are easy to redeem at checkout.

myWalgreens: How the Program Works

Are there items that cannot be purchased to earn or redeem Walgreens Cash rewards?

With the exception of photo orders and prescriptions (which must be picked up in a Participating Store in order to earn Walgreens Cash rewards), items ordered online and delivered to Member’s home will earn Walgreens Cash rewards as they would if purchased in store. Items ordered online using the “store pickup” feature will only earn Walgreens Cash rewards after the items have been picked up from the store. Rewards Promotions will not apply to rain check items purchased after the promotion has ended. Walgreens Cash rewards will not be earned on the portion of a transaction that was paid for using Walgreens Cash rewards or store credit. Unless explicitly stated otherwise, Rewards Promotions and Walgreens Cash rewards cannot be earned on purchases of: alcohol; dairy; tobacco; stamps; phone/prepaid/gift cards; money orders/transfers; transportation passes; lottery tickets; charitable donations; pseudoephedrine or ephedrine products; clinic services; pharmacy items or services covered under Washington state workers’ compensation; pharmacy items or services furnished by a Participating Store located in Arkansas, New Jersey or New York; prescriptions received through mail-order services; prescriptions received through specialty pharmacy services; sales tax; shipping; the Prescription Savings Club membership fee; and certain items that are excluded, in particular Rewards Promotions and any other items specified as exclusions by the Company from time to time or where prohibited by law. Walgreens Cash rewards cannot be earned for transferring a prescription to a Participating Store by a Member who is, or at any time in the prior 6 months was, a beneficiary of Medicare, Medicaid, Tricare or any other healthcare program funded in whole or in part by the federal government; Walgreens Cash rewards will also not be earned for transferring a prescription to a Participating Store located in Alabama, Mississippi, Oregon, Puerto Rico, Tennessee, or Virginia. See store for details or contact a customer service representative at 855-225-9225. Walgreen Co. reserves the right to add or delete items eligible for Walgreens Cash rewards issuance at any time, at its sole discretion, without notice.

Can I combine Walgreens Cash rewards offers with in-store discounts and coupons?

Yes, you may use Walgreens Cash rewards along with other Walgreens or Duane Reade manufacturer discounts or promotions. For more information, view the complete

coupon policy.

Can I earn and redeem Walgreens Cash rewards at Rite Aid stores that are transitioning to a Walgreens store?

Rite Aid stores owned by Walgreens currently do not participate in myWalgreens. For more information visit walgreens.com/RiteAid.

Can I earn and redeem Walgreens Cash rewards on the same purchase?

Yes, but Walgreens Cash rewards will not be earned on the portion of a transaction that was paid for using redemption dollars or store credit..

Does my Walgreens Cash rewards expire?

Yes, Walgreens Cash rewards expire one year after they are earned or if an account has been inactive for 6 months. Your account is deemed inactive if you have not used your membership in store or online.

How do I request credit for Walgreens Cash rewards if they were not added to my account?

After logging into your account at myWalgreens click on Credit for missing Walgreens Cash rewards. You can also access this on your mobile phone via the Walgreens mobile app or by calling (855) 225-0400 to speak with a customer service representative.

I live in a state that cannot earn Walgreens Cash rewards for prescriptions; what else can I earn Walgreens Cash rewards on?

You can earn 1% Walgreens Cash rewards storewide and 5% on Walgreens branded items. This only applies to items with the Walgreens and Corner W brands on the packaging.

If I shop at both Walgreens and Duane Reade, do all my Walgreens Cash rewards accumulate in one account?

Yes, all your Walgreens Cash rewards will accumulate in your myWalgreens account..

Is this program active in Duane Reade and Walgreens stores?

Yes, this program is active at both Duane Reade and Walgreens. You can earn and redeem Walgreens Cash rewards at over 8,000 stores nationwide and on Walgreens.com.

The Balance Rewards for healthy choices® program has ended, but as a myWalgreens™ member you can earn Walgreens Cash rewards for reaching your health goals and enjoy great benefits.

As of April 19, 2022 this feature is no longer available on the Walgreens App, but stay tuned for new features and benefits for myWalgreens members.

Balance® Rewards, Beauty Enthusiast, Balance Rewards for healthy choices®, Walgreens Plus and our AARP partnership have changed as we improve upon these programs with myWalgreens.

Will Beauty Enthusiast members still receive beauty samples and event invites?

Yes! You’ll continue to receive invites to beauty sampling events at your local Walgreens cosmetics department when you join myWalgreens.

myWalgreens: Overview

How does myWalgreens™ benefit customers and patients?

myWalgreens™ makes saving, shopping and your well-being easier. Join free and enjoy the following benefits:

  • Save money by unlocking sale prices.
  • Earn unlimited 1% Walgreens Cash rewards storewide on eligible purchases, even at the pharmacy
  • Earn unlimited 5% Walgreens Cash rewards on Walgreens branded products
  • Earn bonus rewards by achieving health goals
  • Save time with our Pickup service—ready in as little as 30 minutes curbside, drive-thru or in-store when you order online or with our app
  • Quick, contactless checkout is easy with new digital receipts and Wallet
  • Give back to communities you care about by donating your rewards

What is myWalgreens™?

myWalgreens™ is a one-of-a-kind personalized experience that makes saving, shopping and your well-being easier. Designed for the one and only you.

What’s the difference between myWalgreens™ and Balance Rewards?

We kept everything you love about Balance Rewards and made it even better. myWalgreens is more than a rewards program—it makes saving, shopping and your well-being easier. As a member, you’ll enjoy a truly customized experience when you join, including personalized deals, product recommendations, well-being benefits and more ways to get time back. From prescription refills to curbside pickup, our free app makes it faster and easier. Learn more about how myWalgreens compares to Balance Rewards.

Which products qualify for 5% Walgreens Cash rewards on Walgreens branded products?

You can earn 5% Walgreens Cash rewards on all Walgreens branded products. This means anytime you buy a product carrying the Walgreens brand name—including beauty products like facial cleansers or nail polish remover and health essentials like vitamins—you’ll earn unlimited 5% Walgreens Cash rewards.

Will there be a myWalgreens™ card?

myWalgreens does not require a membership card, so you can earn and redeem rewards with less contact. That makes checkout safer for you and our team members, to help everyone stay healthy. It’s also more environmentally friendly, because we care about our communities.

myWalgreens: Redemption

How do I spend Walgreens Cash rewards?

“It’s easy! You can use your Walgreens Cash rewards by providing your phone number or the contactless checkout bar code associated with your account during your purchase—just make sure your email address has been added to your myWalgreens™ account. When you spend your Walgreens Cash rewards, the savings are deducted from the total price of your purchase. Walgreens Cash rewards can be used in addition to Walgreens or other manufacturer discounts or promotions, unless otherwise stated in the promotional offer. Walgreens Cash rewards are applied to eligible purchases after tax and after all discounts, percent-off and manufacturer coupons. Earned Walgreens Cash rewards are converted into savings for you based on the following redemption tiers:

(1) $1 Walgreen Cash rewards is converted to $1

(2) $2 Walgreens Cash rewards is converted to $2

(3) $3 Walgreens Cash rewards is converted to $3

(4) $4 Walgreens Cash rewards is converted to $4

(5) $5 Walgreens Cash rewards is converted to $5

(6) $10 Walgreens Cash rewards is converted to $10

(7) $20 Walgreens Cash rewards is converted to $20

(8) $50 Walgreens Cash rewards is converted to $50

Walgreens Cash rewards can only be redeemed in the increments described above.

How long are my Walgreens Cash rewards valid?

Walgreens Cash rewards expire one year after they are earned or if an account has been inactive for 6 months. Your account is deemed inactive if you have not used your membership in-store or online.

Why was I charged in full for an order that I redeemed Walgreens Cash rewards for?

Your order may not have shipped in full.

myWalgreens: Your Account

If I have more than one myWalgreens account, can I transfer Walgreens Cash rewards between them?

No, you cannot transfer Walgreens Cash rewards between accounts, but you may merge your accounts. Please call (855) 225-0400 and speak with a customer service representative to merge accounts.

When do Walgreens Cash rewards get posted to my myWalgreens account?

In most cases, Walgreens Cash rewards will be posted to your account immediately after your purchase. For online and pharmacy orders, it may take up to 24 hours to be awarded due to account and legal verification.

Where can I find out how many Walgreens Cash rewards I have?

You can see your current Walgreens Cash rewards balance by logging in to your account at myWalgreens. or on the Walgreens mobile app. You can also view your current Walgreens Cash rewards balance on your last Walgreens receipt or by calling (855) 225-0400.

Can I use Apple Pay for orders on Walgreens.com?

Yes, we now accept Apple Pay on the web, in the app, and in our stores. Unfortunately, we do not accept Apple Pay for Photo purchases paid for online.

What is Apple Pay?

Apple Pay is a convenient and secure way to pay for your in-store purchases with your iPhone 6 or iPhone 6 Plus.

Once you connect a credit or debit card from a participating bank to your Apple Pay account, you’ll be able to pay for your purchase at any Walgreens location by simply holding your phone near the reader at checkout and placing your finger over the TouchID sensor on your phone to authorize the payment.

For more information about how Apple Pay works, click here.

Payment Methods: CareCredit

Can I pay for Walgreens items online, through the mobile app, or by phone with my CareCredit card?

Only in-store purchases can be made with CareCredit at this time.

Is CareCredit accepted at all Walgreens and Duane Reade locations?

Yes, you can use your CareCredit card at all Walgreens and Duane Reade locations across the United States. To pay, simply swipe or insert your card as you would any other credit card.

Is Promotional Financing available on purchases from Walgreens?

No. Walgreens purchases currently do not qualify for Promotional Financing on your CareCredit card.

What can I purchase with my CareCredit card at Walgreens?

All items at Walgreens stores except for lottery tickets, gift cards/reloads, alcohol, and tobacco can be purchased.

Payment Methods: Gift Cards

Can I buy Walgreens Gift Cards online?

Yes, you can purchase Walgreens Gift Cards online in select denominations. Walgreens Gift Cards in other amounts can be purchased at your local Walgreens store. Follow Walgreens Cards for more information.

Can I redeem a Gift Card online?

Yes, Walgreens Gift Cards can be redeemed for online purchases. Walgreens Gift Cards can be used in store for purchases.

Do Gift Cards expire or have dormancy fees?

As of September 1, 2005, Walgreens does not apply dormancy fees or expirations to Walgreens Gift Cards or Walgreens Cards. If your card was purchased or loaded prior to September 1, 2005, it is possible that a non-use service fee of $2 per month was applied to the initial remaining balance on the card.

How can I check the balance on my Walgreens Gift Card or Walgreens Card?

What are your policies and restrictions regarding gift cards?
